# D:\Python_Programs\Stewart_Platform\main.py

import sys
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QApplication
from PyQt6.QtGui import QFont, QScreen
from src.gui.main_window import MainWindow 
from src.core import config  # 引入設定檔

def main():
    """應用程式主函數"""
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    
    # 設定應用程式全局字體
    app.setFont(QFont(config.FONT_UI_NAME, config.FONT_UI_SIZE))
    
    main_window = MainWindow()
    
    # 設定主視窗尺寸並置中
    main_window.setGeometry(0, 0, config.DRIVE_DASHBOARD_WIDTH, config.DRIVE_DASHBOARD_HEIGHT)
    screen = app.primaryScreen().geometry()
    main_window.move(
        (screen.width() - config.DRIVE_DASHBOARD_WIDTH) // 2,
        (screen.height() - config.DRIVE_DASHBOARD_HEIGHT) // 2
    )
    main_window.show()

    # 根據 config 決定是否顯示 3D 預覽視窗
    if config.STARTUP_SHOW_3D_PREVIEW:
        pv_window = main_window.pv_window
        pv_window.show()

    sys.exit(app.exec())

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

FILE PATH: prepare_handover.py

import os
import shutil
from datetime import datetime

# ================= 設定區 =================
OUTPUT_DIR = "GEM_Handover_Output"
TARGET_EXTENSIONS = {".py", ".json", ".xml", ".md"} # 要抓取的檔案類型
IGNORE_DIRS = {".venv", "__pycache__", ".git", ".vscode", "GEM_Handover_Output"}
# 指定要特別抓取的測試數據檔名 (請確認您的檔名是否正確)
TEST_DATA_FILENAME = "o-Ride E_平台數據.json" 

# 交接報告內容 (自動生成)
HANDOVER_REPORT_CONTENT = f"""# 專案交接狀態報告
日期: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}
版本: v2.4.1 (動力學與 3D 預覽修復版)

## 1. 專案現況概述
本專案為「史都華平台設計與模擬工具」，使用 Python (PyQt6 + PyVista) 開發。
目前剛完成 v2.4.1 版本的關鍵 Bug 修復，準備進入下一階段開發。

## 2. Git 與版本狀態
- **目前分支**: main (已合併 fix-dynamics-algo 開發分支)
- **最新變更**:
    1. [Core] 修正動力學矩陣逆運算錯誤 (Inv(J^T))，靜態受力回歸物理合理值。
    2. [Core] 修正 6-DOF 幾何求解器在非零相位角下的 Yaw 軸鎖定邏輯。
    3. [GUI] 修正 main_window.py 中的 3D 預覽邏輯，解決平台主體與法線分離問題。

## 3. 檔案結構說明
- `src/core/`: 包含 kinematics, dynamics, config 等核心邏輯。
- `src/gui/`: 包含 main_window 及各個 widget (controls/)。
- `data/`: 存放專案設定檔。

## 4. 待辦事項 (Next Steps)
- 請讀取 `Project_Code_Snapshot.txt` 以理解完整程式碼邏輯。
- 請使用 `o-Ride E_平台數據.json` 進行回歸測試，驗證推力數值 (預期: 60度相位角/200kg負載下約 4781N)。
"""

def generate_tree(startpath):
    """生成目錄結構樹"""
    tree_str = "Project Directory Structure:\n"
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(startpath):
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
        level = root.replace(startpath, '').count(os.sep)
        indent = ' ' * 4 * (level)
        tree_str += '{}{}/\n'.format(indent, os.path.basename(root))
        subindent = ' ' * 4 * (level + 1)
        for f in files:
            if f.endswith(tuple(TARGET_EXTENSIONS)) or f == "requirements.txt":
                tree_str += '{}{}\n'.format(subindent, f)
    return tree_str

def generate_code_snapshot(startpath):
    """將所有程式碼合併為一個檔案"""
    snapshot_content = ""
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(startpath):
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
        for file in files:
            if file.endswith(".py") or file == "requirements.txt":
                file_path = os.path.join(root, file)
                rel_path = os.path.relpath(file_path, startpath)
                
                snapshot_content += f"\n{'='*60}\n"
                snapshot_content += f"FILE PATH: {rel_path}\n"
                snapshot_content += f"{'='*60}\n"
                
                try:
                    with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        snapshot_content += f.read() + "\n"
                except Exception as e:
                    snapshot_content += f"[Error reading file: {e}]\n"
    return snapshot_content

def main():
    # 1. 建立輸出資料夾
    if not os.path.exists(OUTPUT_DIR):
        os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR)
    
    print(f"正在準備轉移文件至 {OUTPUT_DIR}...")

    # 2. 生成目錄結構樹 (1_Directory_Tree.txt)
    tree_content = generate_tree(".")
    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "1_Directory_Tree.txt"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(tree_content)
    print("-> 已生成目錄結構樹。")

    # 3. 生成程式碼快照 (2_Project_Code_Snapshot.txt)
    code_content = generate_code_snapshot(".")
    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "2_Project_Code_Snapshot.txt"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(code_content)
    print("-> 已生成程式碼快照 (包含所有 .py 檔)。")

    # 4. 生成交接報告 (3_Handover_Status_Report.md)
    with open(os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "3_Handover_Status_Report.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(HANDOVER_REPORT_CONTENT)
    print("-> 已生成交接狀態報告。")

    # 5. 複製測試數據 (4_Test_Data.json)
    # 嘗試在專案根目錄或 data/projects 尋找
    found_data = False
    possible_paths = [TEST_DATA_FILENAME, os.path.join("data", "projects", TEST_DATA_FILENAME)]
    
    for path in possible_paths:
        if os.path.exists(path):
            shutil.copy(path, os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "4_Test_Data.json"))
            print(f"-> 已複製測試數據: {path}")
            found_data = True
            break
    
    if not found_data:
        print(f"⚠️ 警告: 找不到 '{TEST_DATA_FILENAME}'。請手動將該測試數據複製到輸出資料夾。")

    print(f"\n✅ 轉移文件準備完成！請將 '{OUTPUT_DIR}' 資料夾內的檔案上傳至新 GEM。")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# D:\Python_Programs\Stewart_Platform\main.py

import sys
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QApplication
from PyQt6.QtGui import QScreen
from src.gui.main_window import MainWindow 

def main():
    """應用程式主函數"""
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    
    # --- [修改] 回歸傳統的視窗建立方式 ---
    main_window = MainWindow()
    pv_window = main_window.pv_window

    # --- 使用 QScreen 以可靠的方式獲取螢幕尺寸 ---
    primary_screen = QScreen.availableGeometry(QApplication.primaryScreen())
    screen_width = primary_screen.width()
    screen_height = primary_screen.height()
    
    # 計算每個視窗的寬度（平均分配）
    window_width = screen_width // 2
    
    # 設定主視窗的位置和大小
    main_window.setGeometry(
        primary_screen.x(), # 螢幕最左邊
        primary_screen.y(), # 螢幕最頂端
        window_width,
        screen_height
    )
    
    # 設定 3D 預覽視窗的位置和大小
    pv_window.setGeometry(
        primary_screen.x() + window_width, # 緊鄰主視窗右側
        primary_screen.y(),
        window_width,
        screen_height
    )
    
    # 顯示兩個視窗
    main_window.show()
    pv_window.show()

    # 啟動應用程式的事件循環
    sys.exit(app.exec())

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

# D:\Python_Programs\Stewart_Platform\test_pybullet_widget.py

import sys
import pybullet as p
import numpy as np
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QApplication, QMainWindow, QWidget
from PyQt6.QtCore import QTimer, Qt
from PyQt6.QtGui import QImage, QPainter

# 這是從您之前的版本中簡化而來的 PyBulletWidget
# 它的唯一職責就是在一個乾淨的環境中繪圖
class PyBulletTestWidget(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.physics_client_id = -1
        self.camera_yaw = 90.0
        self.camera_pitch = -45.0
        self.camera_distance = 2.5
        self.camera_target_pos = [0.0, 0.0, 0.5]
        self.setMinimumSize(800, 600)
        
        # 使用 QTimer 來確保 PyQt 的事件循環和 PyBullet 的渲染有機會協調
        self.timer = QTimer(self)
        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update) # 持續觸發 paintEvent
        self.timer.start(1000 // 60) # 60 FPS
        
        self._init_pybullet()
        self._draw_test_model()

    def _init_pybullet(self):
        if self.physics_client_id < 0:
            # 我們使用 p.GUI 來創建一個獨立的調試窗口，以便對比
            # self.physics_client_id = p.connect(p.GUI) 
            # 如果上面的 p.GUI 能顯示，但下面的 p.DIRECT 不行，就說明問題出在渲染到Qt的環節
            self.physics_client_id = p.connect(p.DIRECT)
            p.configureDebugVisualizer(p.COV_ENABLE_GUI, 0, physicsClientId=self.physics_client_id)
            self._draw_world_frame()

    def _draw_world_frame(self):
        p.addUserDebugLine([0,0,0], [1,0,0], [1,0,0], 2, physicsClientId=self.physics_client_id)
        p.addUserDebugLine([0,0,0], [0,1,0], [0,1,0], 2, physicsClientId=self.physics_client_id)
        p.addUserDebugLine([0,0,0], [0,0,1], [0,0,1], 2, physicsClientId=self.physics_client_id)

    def _draw_test_model(self):
        """使用您截圖中的硬編碼數據來繪製模型"""
        # 這些是從您成功的終端日誌中提取的、未經旋轉的純粹數學座標
        base_nodes = [[1.152, 0.0, 0], [1.13, 0.228, 0], [0.88, 0.74, 0], [0.0, 1.152, 0], [-0.228, 1.13, 0], [-0.74, 0.88, 0]]
        mobile_nodes_local = [[0.939, 0.0, 0], [0.92, 0.186, 0], [0.72, 0.60, 0], [0.0, 0.939, 0], [-0.186, 0.92, 0], [-0.60, 0.72, 0]]
        
        # 繪製下平台
        for i in range(6):
            p1, p2 = base_nodes[i], base_nodes[(i + 1) % 6]
            p.addUserDebugLine(p1, p2, [0.2, 0.6, 1], 3, self.physics_client_id)

        # 假設平台處於零位姿態
        position = [0, 0, 1.363] # H
        orientation_quat = [0, 0, 0, 1]
        rot_matrix = np.array(p.getMatrixFromQuaternion(orientation_quat)).reshape(3, 3)
        mobile_nodes_world = [np.array(position) + rot_matrix @ np.array(node) for node in mobile_nodes_local]
        
        # 繪製上平台
        for i in range(6):
            p1, p2 = mobile_nodes_world[i], mobile_nodes_world[(i + 1) % 6]
            p.addUserDebugLine(p1, p2, [0.2, 0.9, 0.4], 3, self.physics_client_id)
        
        # 繪製連桿
        for i in range(6):
            p_base, p_mobile = base_nodes[i], mobile_nodes_world[i]
            p.addUserDebugLine(p_base, p_mobile, [0.8, 0.8, 0.2], 2, self.physics_client_id)

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        if self.physics_client_id < 0: return

        w, h = self.width(), self.height()
        if h == 0: return

        view_matrix = p.computeViewMatrixFromYawPitchRoll(
            cameraTargetPosition=self.camera_target_pos,
            distance=self.camera_distance,
            yaw=self.camera_yaw,
            pitch=self.camera_pitch,
            roll=0,
            upAxisIndex=2,
            physicsClientId=self.physics_client_id
        )
        proj_matrix = p.computeProjectionMatrixFOV(60, w/h, 0.01, 100.0, self.physics_client_id)
        
        # 這是最關鍵的一步：從PyBullet的渲染緩衝區獲取圖像
        (_, _, rgba, _, _) = p.getCameraImage(w, h, view_matrix, proj_matrix, renderer=p.ER_BULLET_HARDWARE_OPENGL, physicsClientId=self.physics_client_id)
        
        image = QImage(bytes(rgba), w, h, QImage.Format.Format_RGBA8888)
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.drawImage(0, 0, image)

def main():
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    window = QMainWindow()
    test_widget = PyBulletTestWidget()
    window.setCentralWidget(test_widget)
    window.setWindowTitle("PyBullet 獨立渲染測試")
    window.setGeometry(100, 100, 800, 600)
    window.show()
    sys.exit(app.exec())

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

# D:\Python_Programs\Stewart_Platform\src\core\dynamics_engine.py

import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial.transform import Rotation

class DynamicsEngine:
    """
    負責處理史都華平台的逆動力學計算。
    輸入：平台几何、負載參數、期望運動狀態（加速度）。
    輸出：各電動缸所需的瞬時推力/拉力。
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.g = np.array([0, 0, -9.81]) # 重力加速度向量 (m/s^2)
        print("動力學計算引擎 (DynamicsEngine) v1.0 已初始化。")

    def calculate_actuator_forces(self, platform_params: dict, dynamics_params: dict) -> list[float] | None:
        """
        逆動力學求解器的主要入口。

        Args:
            platform_params: 包含平台幾何資訊的字典 (由 CoreEngine 提供)。
            dynamics_params: 包含負載和運動資訊的字典 (由 DynamicsWidget 提供)。

        Returns:
            一個包含六根電動缸所需力的列表 (牛頓)，正值為推力，負值為拉力。
            如果計算失敗則返回 None。
        """
        
        base_nodes = platform_params.get('nodes_3d_base')
        mobile_nodes_world = platform_params.get('nodes_3d_mobile_world')
        
        m_platform = dynamics_params.get('platform_mass', 200.0)
        m_load = dynamics_params.get('load_mass', 0.0)
        
        load_com_local = np.array([
            dynamics_params.get('load_com_x', 0.0),
            dynamics_params.get('load_com_y', 0.0),
            dynamics_params.get('load_com_z', 0.7)
        ])
        
        lin_accel = np.array(dynamics_params.get('lin_accel', [0,0,0]))
        ang_accel = np.array(dynamics_params.get('ang_accel', [0,0,0]))
        
        R = Rotation.from_quat(platform_params.get('orientation_quat', [0,0,0,1])).as_matrix()
        T = np.array(platform_params.get('position', [0,0,0]))

        if base_nodes is None or mobile_nodes_world is None:
            return None

        m_total = m_platform + m_load
        
        platform_com_world = T
        load_com_world = T + R @ load_com_local
        
        total_com_world = (m_platform * platform_com_world + m_load * load_com_world) / m_total

        leg_vectors = mobile_nodes_world - np.array(base_nodes)
        leg_unit_vectors = leg_vectors / np.linalg.norm(leg_vectors, axis=1)[:, np.newaxis]
        
        r_vectors = mobile_nodes_world - total_com_world
        
        wrench_matrix = np.zeros((6, 6))
        for i in range(6):
            wrench_matrix[:3, i] = leg_unit_vectors[i]
            wrench_matrix[3:, i] = np.cross(r_vectors[i], leg_unit_vectors[i])

        try:
            J_inv = np.linalg.inv(wrench_matrix.T)
        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:
            print("錯誤：雅可比矩陣奇異，無法求解。")
            return None

        force_g = m_total * self.g
        torque_g = np.cross(total_com_world, force_g)
        
        I_total_approx = np.eye(3) * 0.1
        
        force_inertia = -m_total * lin_accel
        torque_inertia = -(I_total_approx @ ang_accel)
        
        total_wrench = np.concatenate([
            force_g + force_inertia,
            torque_g + torque_inertia
        ])

        actuator_forces = J_inv @ (-total_wrench)

        return actuator_forces.tolist()

# D:\Python_Programs\Stewart_Platform\src\core\kinematics.py

import numpy as np
import json
from scipy.optimize import least_squares, fsolve, minimize
from scipy.spatial.transform import Rotation

from . import config

class CoreEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.params = {}
        self.formulas = []
        self.platform_type = '6-DOF'
        self.zero_pose_offset = {'x': 0.0, 'y': 0.0}
        self._initialize_parameters()
        self._register_formulas()
        print("核心計算引擎 v8.0 (最終工作空間修正) 已初始化。")

    def _initialize_parameters(self):
        self.params.clear()
        geom_keys_6dof = ['Df', 'df', 'Dm', 'dm']
        geom_keys_3dof = ['D1', 'D2', 'd1', 'd2']
        geom_keys_common = ['Ra', 'Rb', 'H', 'L', 's_mech', 's_buffer', 's']
        for key in geom_keys_6dof + geom_keys_3dof + geom_keys_common:
            self.params[key] = 0.0
        self.params['view_params'] = None
        dyn_keys = {'platform_mass': 200.0, 'load_mass': 500.0, 'load_com_x': 0.0, 'load_com_y': 0.0, 'load_com_z': 0.7, 'lin_accel': [0.0, 0.0, 0.5 * 9.81], 'ang_accel': [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]}
        self.params.update(dyn_keys)

    def _register_formulas(self):
        self.formulas.clear()
        self.formulas.append({'output': 'Ra', 'inputs': ['Df', 'df'], 'func': self._calculate_radius_from_chords, 'type': '6-DOF'})
        self.formulas.append({'output': 'Rb', 'inputs': ['Dm', 'dm'], 'func': self._calculate_radius_from_chords, 'type': '6-DOF'})
        self.formulas.append({'output': 'Ra', 'inputs': ['D1', 'D2'], 'func': self._calculate_radius_from_3dof_triangle, 'type': '3-DOF'})
        self.formulas.append({'output': 'Rb', 'inputs': ['d1', 'd2'], 'func': self._calculate_radius_from_3dof_triangle, 'type': '3-DOF'})
        self.formulas.append({'output': 's_mech', 'inputs': ['s', 's_buffer'], 'func': lambda p: p.get('s', 0) + p.get('s_buffer', 0), 'type': 'common'})

    def update_parameter(self, name, value):
        self.params[name] = value

    def get_parameter(self, name):
        return self.params.get(name)

    def get_all_parameters(self):
        all_params = self.params.copy()
        all_params['zero_pose_offset_x'] = self.zero_pose_offset.get('x', 0.0)
        all_params['zero_pose_offset_y'] = self.zero_pose_offset.get('y', 0.0)
        return all_params

    def load_parameters(self, data: dict):
        self._initialize_parameters()
        self.platform_type = data.get('platform_type', '6-DOF')
        for key, value in data.items():
            if key in self.params:
                self.params[key] = value
        self.zero_pose_offset['x'] = data.get('zero_pose_offset_x', 0.0)
        self.zero_pose_offset['y'] = data.get('zero_pose_offset_y', 0.0)
        print(f"參數已從檔案載入。平台類型: {self.platform_type}")

    def save_to_file(self, filepath: str):
        try:
            data_to_save = self.get_all_parameters()
            data_to_save['platform_type'] = self.platform_type
            with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(data_to_save, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"存檔失敗: {e}"); return False

    def load_from_file(self, filepath: str):
        try:
            with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f: data = json.load(f)
            self.load_parameters(data)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"讀檔失敗: {e}"); return False

    def calculate_target(self, target_name):
        for formula in self.formulas:
            is_common = formula['type'] == 'common'
            type_match = formula['type'] == self.platform_type
            if formula['output'] == target_name and (is_common or type_match):
                if not all(k in self.params for k in formula['inputs']): continue
                input_params = {k: self.params[k] for k in formula['inputs']}
                result = formula['func'](input_params)
                if result is not None: self.update_parameter(target_name, result); return result
        return None

    def calculate_platform_geometry(self):
        if self.platform_type == '6-DOF':
            return self._calculate_geometry_6dof()
        elif self.platform_type == '3-DOF':
            return self._calculate_geometry_3dof()
        return False

    def _get_canonical_nodes(self):
        if self.platform_type == '6-DOF':
            return self._get_6dof_nodes()
        elif self.platform_type == '3-DOF':
            return self._get_3dof_nodes()
        return [], []

    def calculate_initial_height(self) -> float | None:
        required_params = ['L', 'Ra', 'Rb']
        if not all(self.get_parameter(p) and self.get_parameter(p) > 0 for p in required_params): return None
        base_nodes, mobile_nodes = self._get_canonical_nodes()
        if not base_nodes or not mobile_nodes: return None
        target_leg_length = self.get_parameter('L')
        if self.platform_type == '6-DOF':
            def error_func(pose_vars):
                Tx, Ty, Tz, pitch, roll, yaw = pose_vars
                r = Rotation.from_euler('ZXY', [yaw, pitch, roll], degrees=False)
                errors = []
                for i in range(len(base_nodes)):
                    Ai, Bi = np.array(base_nodes[i]), np.array(mobile_nodes[i])
                    li_vec = (np.array([Tx, Ty, Tz]) + r.apply(Bi)) - Ai
                    errors.append(np.linalg.norm(li_vec)**2 - target_leg_length**2)
                return errors
            initial_guess = [0, 0, target_leg_length, 0, 0, 0]
            result = least_squares(error_func, initial_guess, method='lm', ftol=config.GEOMETRY_SOLVER_TOLERANCE)
            return result.x[2] if result.success else None
        else:
            def error_func_3dof(H_var):
                H = H_var[0]
                errors = []
                for i in range(len(base_nodes)):
                    Ai, Bi = np.array(base_nodes[i]), np.array(mobile_nodes[i])
                    li_vec = (np.array([0, 0, H]) + Bi) - Ai
                    errors.append(np.linalg.norm(li_vec)**2 - target_leg_length**2)
                return np.sum(np.array(errors)**2)
            result = minimize(error_func_3dof, [target_leg_length], method='SLSQP', bounds=[(0, None)])
            return result.x[0] if result.success else None

    def get_phase_angle_deg(self) -> float | None:
        if self.platform_type != '6-DOF': return 0.0
        Ra, Df, df = self.get_parameter('Ra'), self.get_parameter('Df'), self.get_parameter('df')
        Rb, Dm, dm = self.get_parameter('Rb'), self.get_parameter('Dm'), self.get_parameter('dm')
        if not all([Ra, Df, df, Rb, Dm, dm]) or Df <= df or Dm <= dm: return None
        try:
            if Ra <= 0 or Rb <= 0: return None
            if Df / (2 * Ra) > 1 or df / (2 * Ra) > 1 or Dm / (2 * Rb) > 1 or dm / (2 * Rb) > 1: return None
            beta_f = 2 * np.arcsin(df / (2 * Ra))
            alpha_m = 2 * np.arcsin(Dm / (2 * Rb))
            return np.rad2deg((beta_f - alpha_m) / 2)
        except (ValueError, TypeError, ZeroDivisionError):
            return None

    def _calculate_radius_from_chords(self, p):
        D_chord, d_chord = (p.get('Df'), p.get('df')) if 'Df' in p else (p.get('Dm'), p.get('dm'))
        if not D_chord or not d_chord or D_chord <= d_chord or d_chord <= 0: return None
        def equations(x):
            R, alpha = x
            if R <= 0 or alpha <= 0 or alpha >= np.pi: return [1e6, 1e6]
            beta = (2 * np.pi / 3) - alpha
            if beta <= 0 or beta >= np.pi: return [1e6, 1e6]
            return [D_chord - 2 * R * np.sin(alpha / 2), d_chord - 2 * R * np.sin(beta / 2)]
        try:
            solution, _, ier, _ = fsolve(equations, [D_chord, np.pi/3], full_output=True)
            return float(solution[0]) if ier == 1 else None
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return None

    def _calculate_radius_from_3dof_triangle(self, p):
        base = p.get('D1') if 'D1' in p else p.get('d1')
        height = p.get('D2') if 'D2' in p else p.get('d2')
        if not base or not height or base <= 0 or height <= 0: return None
        try:
            side_a_sq = (base / 2)**2 + height**2
            side_a = side_a_sq**0.5
            area = 0.5 * base * height
            if area <= 0: return None
            radius = (side_a * side_a * base) / (4 * area)
            return radius
        except (ValueError, ZeroDivisionError):
            return None
    
    def _get_3dof_nodes(self):
        base_nodes, mobile_nodes = [], []
        D1, D2 = self.get_parameter('D1'), self.get_parameter('D2')
        if D1 and D2 and D1 > 0 and D2 > 0:
            y_offset = D2 / 2 - (D1**2) / (8 * D2) if D2 > 0 else 0
            p3 = [0, D2 - y_offset, 0]
            p1 = [-D1 / 2, -y_offset, 0]
            p5 = [D1 / 2, -y_offset, 0]
            base_nodes = [p1, p3, p5]
            
        d1, d2 = self.get_parameter('d1'), self.get_parameter('d2')
        if d1 and d2 and d1 > 0 and d2 > 0:
            y_offset_m = d2 / 2 - (d1**2) / (8 * d2) if d2 > 0 else 0
            m3 = [0, d2 - y_offset_m, 0]
            m1 = [-d1 / 2, -y_offset_m, 0]
            m5 = [d1 / 2, -y_offset_m, 0]
            mobile_nodes = [m1, m3, m5]
            
        return base_nodes, mobile_nodes

    def _calculate_geometry_3dof(self):
        required = ['L', 's', 's_buffer', 'Ra', 'Rb']
        if not all(self.get_parameter(p) is not None for p in required): return False
        
        base_nodes, mobile_nodes = self._get_3dof_nodes()
        if len(base_nodes) != 3 or len(mobile_nodes) != 3: return False
        
        l_val = self.get_parameter('L')
        s_val = self.get_parameter('s')
        s_buffer_val = self.get_parameter('s_buffer')
        target_leg_length = l_val + s_buffer_val / 2.0 + s_val / 2.0

        def error_func(H_var):
            H = H_var[0]
            errors = []
            for i in range(3):
                Ai, Bi = np.array(base_nodes[i]), np.array(mobile_nodes[i])
                li_vec = (np.array([0, 0, H]) + Bi) - Ai
                errors.append(np.linalg.norm(li_vec)**2 - target_leg_length**2)
            return np.sum(np.array(errors)**2)
            
        result = minimize(error_func, [target_leg_length], method='SLSQP', bounds=[(0, None)], tol=config.GEOMETRY_SOLVER_TOLERANCE)
        if result.success:
            self.update_parameter('H', result.x[0])
            self.zero_pose_offset = {'x': 0.0, 'y': 0.0}
            return True
        return False

    def _calculate_geometry_6dof(self):
        required = ['L', 's', 's_buffer', 's_mech', 'Ra', 'Rb']
        if not all(self.get_parameter(p) is not None and self.get_parameter(p) > 0 for p in required): return False
        
        base_nodes, mobile_nodes = self._get_6dof_nodes()
        if not base_nodes or not mobile_nodes: return False

        l_val = self.get_parameter('L')
        s_val = self.get_parameter('s')
        s_buffer_val = self.get_parameter('s_buffer')
        target_leg_length = l_val + s_buffer_val / 2.0 + s_val / 2.0

        def error_func(pose_vars):
            Tx, Ty, Tz, pitch, roll, yaw = pose_vars
            r = Rotation.from_euler('ZXY', [yaw, pitch, roll], degrees=False)
            errors = []
            for i in range(6):
                Ai, Bi = np.array(base_nodes[i]), np.array(mobile_nodes[i])
                li_vec = (np.array([Tx, Ty, Tz]) + r.apply(Bi)) - Ai
                errors.append(np.linalg.norm(li_vec)**2 - target_leg_length**2)
            return errors
            
        initial_guess = [0, 0, target_leg_length, 0, 0, 0]
        result = least_squares(error_func, initial_guess, method='lm', ftol=config.GEOMETRY_SOLVER_TOLERANCE)
        if result.success:
            solved_pose = result.x; self.update_parameter('H', solved_pose[2])
            self.zero_pose_offset = {'x': solved_pose[0], 'y': solved_pose[1]}; return True
        return False

    def _get_6dof_nodes(self):
        base_nodes, mobile_nodes = [], []
        Ra, Df, df = self.get_parameter('Ra'), self.get_parameter('Df'), self.get_parameter('df')
        rotation_offset = 0 
        if Ra and Ra > 0 and Df and Df > 0 and df is not None and Df > df:
            try:
                if Df / (2 * Ra) > 1 or df / (2 * Ra) > 1: return [], []
                alpha = 2 * np.arcsin(Df / (2 * Ra)); beta = 2 * np.arcsin(df / (2 * Ra))
                angles_base_raw = [0, beta, beta + alpha, beta + alpha + beta, beta + alpha + beta + alpha, beta + alpha + beta + alpha + beta]
                midpoint_angle = (angles_base_raw[0] + angles_base_raw[5]) / 2.0
                rotation_offset = -np.pi/2 - midpoint_angle + np.pi
                angles_base = [a + rotation_offset for a in angles_base_raw]
                base_nodes = [[Ra * np.cos(a), Ra * np.sin(a), 0] for a in angles_base]
            except (ValueError, ZeroDivisionError):
                return [], []
                
        Rb, Dm, dm = self.get_parameter('Rb'), self.get_parameter('Dm'), self.get_parameter('dm')
        if Rb and Rb > 0 and Dm and Dm > 0 and dm is not None and Dm > dm and Ra and df is not None:
            try:
                if Dm / (2 * Rb) > 1 or dm / (2 * Rb) > 1: return [], []
                beta_f = 2 * np.arcsin(df / (2*Ra)) if Ra > 0 and df > 0 and df/(2*Ra) <= 1 else 0
                alpha_m = 2 * np.arcsin(Dm / (2 * Rb)); beta_m = 2 * np.arcsin(dm / (2 * Rb))
                stagger_offset = (beta_f - alpha_m) / 2
                angles_mobile_standard = [stagger_offset, stagger_offset + alpha_m, stagger_offset + alpha_m + beta_m, stagger_offset + alpha_m + beta_m + alpha_m, stagger_offset + alpha_m + beta_m + alpha_m + beta_m, stagger_offset + alpha_m + beta_m + alpha_m + beta_m + alpha_m]
                angles_mobile = [a + rotation_offset for a in angles_mobile_standard]
                mobile_nodes = [[Rb * np.cos(a), Rb * np.sin(a), 0] for a in angles_mobile]
            except (ValueError, ZeroDivisionError):
                return [], []
                
        return base_nodes, mobile_nodes
        
    def _calculate_leg_lengths_6dof(self, pose):
        Tx, Ty, Tz, pitch, roll, yaw = pose
        r = Rotation.from_euler('ZXY', [yaw, pitch, roll], degrees=False)
        base_nodes, mobile_nodes_local = self._get_canonical_nodes()
        if not base_nodes or not mobile_nodes_local or len(base_nodes) != 6: return None
        mobile_nodes_world = r.apply(mobile_nodes_local) + np.array([Tx, Ty, Tz])
        return np.linalg.norm(mobile_nodes_world - np.array(base_nodes), axis=1)

    def _find_6dof_limit(self, dof_index, direction, neutral_pose, leg_min, leg_max):
        objective_func = lambda pose: -direction * pose[dof_index]
        constraints = ({'type': 'ineq', 'fun': lambda p: self._calculate_leg_lengths_6dof(p) - leg_min}, {'type': 'ineq', 'fun': lambda p: leg_max - self._calculate_leg_lengths_6dof(p)})
        result = minimize(objective_func, x0=neutral_pose, method='SLSQP', constraints=constraints, options={'ftol': config.WORKSPACE_SOLVER_TOLERANCE, 'disp': False})
        return result.x[dof_index] if result.success else neutral_pose[dof_index]

    def _analyze_workspace_6dof(self, leg_min, leg_max):
        H, offset = self.get_parameter('H'), self.zero_pose_offset
        if not H or H <= 0: return None
        neutral_pose = np.array([offset['x'], offset['y'], H, 0, 0, 0])
        dof_names = ['x', 'y', 'z', 'pitch', 'roll', 'yaw']; limits = {}
        print(f"開始分析 6-DOF 工作空間 (連桿範圍: {leg_min:.3f}m - {leg_max:.3f}m)...")
        for i, name in enumerate(dof_names):
            min_val = self._find_6dof_limit(i, -1, neutral_pose, leg_min, leg_max)
            max_val = self._find_6dof_limit(i, 1, neutral_pose, leg_min, leg_max)
            if i >= 3: limits[f"{name}_min"], limits[f"{name}_max"] = np.rad2deg(min_val), np.rad2deg(max_val)
            else: limits[f"{name}_min"], limits[f"{name}_max"] = min_val, max_val
        print("6-DOF 工作空間分析完成。"); return limits

    def _calculate_leg_lengths_3dof(self, pose):
        z, pitch, roll = pose
        r = Rotation.from_euler('ZXY', [0, pitch, roll], degrees=False) 
        base_nodes, mobile_nodes_local = self._get_canonical_nodes()
        if not base_nodes or not mobile_nodes_local or len(base_nodes) != 3: return None
        
        mobile_nodes_np = np.array(mobile_nodes_local)
        centroid = np.mean(mobile_nodes_np, axis=0)
        
        nodes_centered = mobile_nodes_np - centroid
        nodes_rotated = r.apply(nodes_centered)
        nodes_translated_back = nodes_rotated + centroid
        
        H0 = self.get_parameter('H')
        position = np.array([0, 0, H0 + z])
        mobile_nodes_world = nodes_translated_back + position
        
        return np.linalg.norm(mobile_nodes_world - np.array(base_nodes), axis=1)

    def _find_3dof_limit(self, dof_name, direction, leg_min, leg_max):
        dof_map = {'z': 0, 'pitch': 1, 'roll': 2}
        dof_index = dof_map[dof_name]
        
        def objective_func(pose):
            return -direction * pose[dof_index]
        
        constraints = ({'type': 'ineq', 'fun': lambda p: self._calculate_leg_lengths_3dof(p) - leg_min},
                       {'type': 'ineq', 'fun': lambda p: leg_max - self._calculate_leg_lengths_3dof(p)})
        
        initial_guess = [0, 0, 0]
        result = minimize(objective_func, x0=initial_guess, method='SLSQP', constraints=constraints, options={'ftol': config.WORKSPACE_SOLVER_TOLERANCE, 'disp': False})
        return result.x[dof_index] if result.success else 0.0

    def _analyze_workspace_3dof(self, leg_min, leg_max):
        if not self.get_parameter('H'): return None
        limits = {}
        print(f"開始分析 3-DOF 工作空間 (連桿範圍: {leg_min:.3f}m - {leg_max:.3f}m)...")
        dof_names = ['z', 'pitch', 'roll']
        for name in dof_names:
            min_val = self._find_3dof_limit(name, -1, leg_min, leg_max)
            max_val = self._find_3dof_limit(name, 1, leg_min, leg_max)
            if name == 'z':
                limits[f"{name}_min"], limits[f"{name}_max"] = min_val * 1000.0, max_val * 1000.0
            else:
                limits[f"{name}_min"], limits[f"{name}_max"] = np.rad2deg(min_val), np.rad2deg(max_val)
        print("3-DOF 工作空間分析完成。"); return limits

    def analyze_operational_workspace(self):
        L = self.get_parameter('L')
        s = self.get_parameter('s')
        s_buffer = self.get_parameter('s_buffer')
        if L is None or s is None or s_buffer is None: return None
        
        leg_min = L + s_buffer / 2.0
        leg_max = leg_min + s
        
        # [新增] 除錯訊息
        print("\n" + "="*50)
        print("開始分析 [可用工作空間 (Operational)]")
        print(f"電動缸長度約束範圍: [{leg_min:.4f} m] - [{leg_max:.4f} m]")
        print("="*50)

        if self.platform_type == '6-DOF':
            return self._analyze_workspace_6dof(leg_min, leg_max)
        elif self.platform_type == '3-DOF':
            return self._analyze_workspace_3dof(leg_min, leg_max)
        return None

    def analyze_mechanical_workspace(self):
        L = self.get_parameter('L')
        s_mech = self.get_parameter('s_mech')
        if L is None or s_mech is None: return None
        
        leg_min = L
        leg_max = L + s_mech

        # [新增] 除錯訊息
        print("\n" + "="*50)
        print("開始分析 [機械極限空間 (Mechanical)]")
        print(f"電動缸長度約束範圍: [{leg_min:.4f} m] - [{leg_max:.4f} m]")
        print("="*50)

        if self.platform_type == '6-DOF':
            return self._analyze_workspace_6dof(leg_min, leg_max)
        elif self.platform_type == '3-DOF':
            return self._analyze_workspace_3dof(leg_min, leg_max)
        return None

# D:\Python_Programs\Stewart_Platform\src\gui\geometry_widget.py

from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QFormLayout, QGroupBox, QPushButton, 
                             QLabel, QDoubleSpinBox)
from ..core import config

class GeometryWidget(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.inputs = {}
        self.labels = {}
        self.buttons = {}
        
    def _create_input_row(self, name, label_text, form_layout):
        spinbox = QDoubleSpinBox(self)
        spinbox.setDecimals(2)
        spinbox.setSingleStep(config.DEFAULT_INPUT_STEP)
        spinbox.setRange(*config.DEFAULT_INPUT_RANGE)
        spinbox.setObjectName(name)
        self.inputs[name] = spinbox
        form_layout.addRow(label_text, spinbox)

    def create_base_group(self):
        group = QGroupBox("下平台", self)
        form = QFormLayout(group)
        self._create_input_row("Df", "較大弦長 (Df, mm):", form)
        self._create_input_row("df", "較小弦長 (df, mm):", form)
        self.labels['Ra'] = QLabel("N/A", self); self.labels['Ra'].setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold;")
        form.addRow("平台半徑 (Ra, mm):", self.labels['Ra'])
        return group

    def create_mobile_group(self):
        group = QGroupBox("上平台", self)
        form = QFormLayout(group)
        self._create_input_row("Dm", "較大弦長 (Dm, mm):", form)
        self._create_input_row("dm", "較小弦長 (dm, mm):", form)
        self.labels['Rb'] = QLabel("N/A", self); self.labels['Rb'].setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold;")
        form.addRow("平台半徑 (Rb, mm):", self.labels['Rb'])
        return group

    def create_actuator_group(self):
        group = QGroupBox("通用與電動缸參數", self)
        form = QFormLayout(group)
        self._create_input_row("L", "最小長度 (L, mm):", form)
        self._create_input_row("s", "可用行程 (s, mm):", form)
        self._create_input_row("s_buffer", "安全裕量 (s_buffer, mm):", form)
        self.labels['s_mech'] = QLabel("N/A", self); self.labels['s_mech'].setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold;")
        form.addRow("總機械行程 (s_mech, mm):", self.labels['s_mech'])
        return group

    def create_calculation_group(self):
        group = QGroupBox("平台計算與分析", self)
        form = QFormLayout(group)
        
        self.buttons['confirm_all'] = QPushButton("確認所有幾何參數", self)
        form.addRow(self.buttons['confirm_all'])
        
        self.labels['H'] = QLabel("N/A", self); self.labels['H'].setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold;")
        form.addRow("零位高度 (H, mm):", self.labels['H'])
        
        self.labels['h_initial'] = QLabel("N/A", self); self.labels['h_initial'].setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; color: #555;")
        form.addRow("初始高度 (h, mm):", self.labels['h_initial'])

        self.labels['phase_angle'] = QLabel("N/A", self); self.labels['phase_angle'].setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; color: #555;")
        form.addRow("幾何相位角 (Δθ, °):", self.labels['phase_angle'])
        
        self.buttons['H'] = QPushButton("計算零位", self); self.buttons['H'].setEnabled(False)
        form.addRow(self.buttons['H'])
        
        self.buttons['analyze_op'] = QPushButton("分析可用工作空間", self); self.buttons['analyze_op'].setEnabled(False)
        form.addRow(self.buttons['analyze_op'])
        
        self.buttons['analyze_mech'] = QPushButton("分析機械極限空間", self); self.buttons['analyze_mech'].setEnabled(False)
        form.addRow(self.buttons['analyze_mech'])
        return group
    
    def get_all_inputs(self):
        params = {}
        for name, spinbox in self.inputs.items(): params[name] = spinbox.value() / 1000.0
        return params

    def update_display_value(self, name, value, unit='mm'):
        if name not in self.labels: return
        
        if value is None or value < -1e9:
            display_text = "N/A"
        elif unit == 'mm':
            value_mm = value * 1000.0
            display_text = f"{value_mm:.2f}"
        elif unit == 'deg':
            display_text = f"{value:.3f}"
        else:
            display_text = f"{value:.2f}"

        self.labels[name].setText(display_text)

    def set_input_values(self, params_m: dict):
        for name, value_m in params_m.items():
            if name in self.inputs and value_m is not None:
                self.inputs[name].blockSignals(True)
                self.inputs[name].setValue(value_m * 1000.0)
                self.inputs[name].blockSignals(False)

import numpy as np

from ..core import config

class MainWindow(QMainWindow):
    BASE_TITLE = "史都華平台設計與模擬工具"

        self.current_project_path = None
        self._is_loading = False

        bottom_buttons_layout.addStretch()

        main_layout.addLayout(bottom_buttons_layout)

        new_btn.clicked.connect(self.on_new_project); save_btn.clicked.connect(self.on_save_project); load_btn.clicked.connect(self.on_load_project)
        self.toggle_3d_btn.clicked.connect(self.toggle_3d_preview_window)
        exit_button.clicked.connect(self.close)

    def _create_view_controls_group(self) -> QGroupBox:
        group = QGroupBox("視角控制"); layout = QHBoxLayout(group)
        layout.setContentsMargins(10, 10, 10, 10); layout.setSpacing(10)
        buttons_info = [("iso", QStyle.StandardPixmap.SP_ComputerIcon, "等角視角 (Isometric)"), ("xy", QStyle.StandardPixmap.SP_ArrowUp, "俯視角 (Top/XY Plane)"), ("xz", QStyle.StandardPixmap.SP_ArrowForward, "正視角 (Front/XZ Plane)"), ("yz", QStyle.StandardPixmap.SP_ArrowRight, "側視角 (Side/YZ Plane)"), ("undo", QStyle.StandardPixmap.SP_FileDialogBack, "撤銷上一步視角 (Undo)"), ("previous", QStyle.StandardPixmap.SP_DialogResetButton, "返回探索前視角 (Previous)"), ("restore_saved", QStyle.StandardPixmap.SP_BrowserReload, "恢復儲存視角 (Restore)")]
        for signal_str, icon_enum, tooltip in buttons_info:
            button = QPushButton(group); icon = self.style().standardIcon(icon_enum)
            button.setIcon(icon); button.setIconSize(QSize(24, 24)); button.setToolTip(tooltip)
            button.clicked.connect(lambda _, s=signal_str: self._on_view_button_clicked(s))
            layout.addWidget(button)
        layout.addStretch()
        return group

    def connect_signals(self):
        self.master_geometry_widget.platform_type_changed.connect(self.on_platform_type_changed)
        self.master_geometry_widget.parameters_confirmed.connect(self.on_parameters_confirmed)
        self.master_geometry_widget.calculation_requested.connect(self.on_calculation_requested)
        self.master_geometry_widget.parameter_changed.connect(self._mark_as_dirty)

        self.master_geometry_widget.op_workspace_analysis_requested.connect(
            lambda: self.on_workspace_analysis_requested('operational')
        )
        self.master_geometry_widget.mech_workspace_analysis_requested.connect(
            lambda: self.on_workspace_analysis_requested('mechanical')
        )

        self.six_dof_controls.pose_changed.connect(self.on_pose_changed)
        self.three_dof_controls.pose_changed.connect(self.on_pose_changed)

        self.pv_window.view_changed.connect(self._mark_as_dirty)
        self.dynamics_widget.analysis_requested.connect(self.on_dynamics_analysis_requested)
        
    def on_platform_type_changed(self, platform_type: str):
        if self._is_loading: return
        if hasattr(self, 'core_engine') and self.core_engine.platform_type == platform_type: return
             
        is_6dof = (platform_type == "6-DOF")
        
        self.controls_stack.setCurrentIndex(0 if is_6dof else 1)
        self.dynamics_widget.setEnabled(is_6dof)
        
        if not is_6dof:
            self.status_label.setText("3-DOF 平台的分析功能已啟用。")
        
        self.on_new_project(platform_type=platform_type)

    def on_new_project(self, platform_type=None):
        if platform_type is None:
            # Check if core_engine exists before trying to access its attributes
            if hasattr(self, 'core_engine') and self.core_engine:
                platform_type = self.core_engine.platform_type
            else:
                platform_type = '6-DOF'

        self.core_engine = CoreEngine()
        self.dynamics_engine = DynamicsEngine()
        self.core_engine.platform_type = platform_type
        
        all_params = self.core_engine.get_all_parameters()
        
        self.master_geometry_widget.set_platform_type(platform_type)
        current_widget = self.master_geometry_widget.get_current_platform_widget()
        current_widget.set_input_values(all_params)
        current_widget.reset_all_calculated_values()
        
        self.dynamics_widget.set_values(all_params)
        
        self.six_dof_controls.reset_workspace_display()
        self.three_dof_controls.reset_workspace_display()

        self.pv_window.clear_and_init_scene()
        self.current_project_path = None
        self._set_clean()
        self.status_label.setText(f"已建立新專案 ({platform_type})。")

    def on_parameters_confirmed(self, params: dict):
        for name, value in params.items(): self.core_engine.update_parameter(name, value)
        
        valid_ra = self.core_engine.calculate_target('Ra') is not None
        valid_rb = self.core_engine.calculate_target('Rb') is not None
        
        current_widget = self.master_geometry_widget.get_current_platform_widget()
        current_widget.update_display_value('Ra', self.core_engine.get_parameter('Ra'))
        current_widget.update_display_value('Rb', self.core_engine.get_parameter('Rb'))
        
        valid_s_mech = self.core_engine.calculate_target('s_mech') is not None
        current_widget.update_display_value('s_mech', self.core_engine.get_parameter('s_mech'))

        can_calc_h = all([valid_ra, valid_rb, valid_s_mech])
        
        buttons = current_widget.get_buttons()
        if 'H' in buttons:
            buttons['H'].setEnabled(can_calc_h)

        if can_calc_h: self.status_label.setText("參數已確認，可計算零位。")

    def on_calculation_requested(self, from_load: bool = False):
        if not from_load:
            success = self.core_engine.calculate_platform_geometry()
            if not success:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "求解失敗", "無法計算零位高度 H，請檢查參數。")
                return
        
        h_val = self.core_engine.get_parameter('H')
        current_widget = self.master_geometry_widget.get_current_platform_widget()
        
        if h_val and h_val > 0:
            current_widget.update_display_value('H', h_val, 'mm')
            
            initial_h_val = self.core_engine.calculate_initial_height()
            current_widget.update_display_value('h_initial', initial_h_val, 'mm')

            if self.core_engine.platform_type == '6-DOF':
                phase_angle_val = self.core_engine.get_phase_angle_deg()
                current_widget.update_display_value('phase_angle', phase_angle_val, 'deg')
            
            QApplication.processEvents()
            buttons = current_widget.get_buttons()
            if 'analyze_op' in buttons: buttons['analyze_op'].setEnabled(True)
            if 'analyze_mech' in buttons: buttons['analyze_mech'].setEnabled(True)
            
            self.on_workspace_analysis_requested('operational')

            if not from_load:
                self.status_label.setText(f"零位求解完成! H = {h_val*1000:.2f} mm")
                is_first_build = not any(actor for name, actor in self.pv_window.platform_actors.items() if name not in ['base_center'])
                self.update_3d_view(rebuild=True, reset_view=is_first_build)
                self._mark_as_dirty()
        elif not from_load:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "求解失敗", "無法計算零位高度 H，請檢查參數。")

    def on_workspace_analysis_requested(self, analysis_type: str):
        print("\n" + "#"*50)
        print(f"主視窗接收到分析請求: '{analysis_type}'")
        print("#"*50)
        
        text = '可用' if analysis_type == 'operational' else '機械極限'
        self.status_label.setText(f"正在分析 {text} 空間，請稍候..."); QApplication.processEvents()
        
        if analysis_type == 'operational':
            limits = self.core_engine.analyze_operational_workspace()
        else:
            limits = self.core_engine.analyze_mechanical_workspace()

        if limits:
            current_controls = self.controls_stack.currentWidget()
            current_controls.update_workspace_display(limits, analysis_type, self.core_engine.get_parameter('H'), self.core_engine.zero_pose_offset)
            
            self.status_label.setText(f"{text} 空間分析完成。")
            self.update_3d_view(rebuild=False)
            self._mark_as_dirty()
        else:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "分析失敗", "無法執行分析，請先成功計算零位。")
            self.status_label.setText("分析失敗。")

    def update_3d_view(self, override_pose: dict = None, rebuild: bool = False, reset_view: bool = False):
        if not self.pv_window.isVisible() and not rebuild: return
        base_nodes, mobile_nodes = self.core_engine._get_canonical_nodes()
        if not base_nodes or not mobile_nodes:
            self.pv_window.clear_and_init_scene()
            return
        platform_params = {'nodes_3d_base': base_nodes, 'nodes_3d_mobile': mobile_nodes}
        labels = {}
        if self.core_engine.platform_type == '6-DOF':
            labels['base'] = [f"A{i+1}" for i in range(len(base_nodes))]
            labels['mobile'] = [f"B{i+1}" for i in range(len(mobile_nodes))]
        elif self.core_engine.platform_type == '3-DOF':
            labels['base'] = ['A1', 'A3', 'A5']
            labels['mobile'] = ['B1', 'B3', 'B5']
        h_val = self.core_engine.get_parameter('H')
        if not h_val or h_val <= 0:
            if rebuild: self.pv_window.rebuild_scene(platform_params, {}, labels, reset_view)
            return

        current_controls = self.controls_stack.currentWidget()
        pose_ui = override_pose if override_pose else {name: ctrl['slider'].value()/config.SLIDER_PRECISION_FACTOR for name, ctrl in current_controls.sliders.items()}

        pitch_from_ui = pose_ui.get('pitch', 0)
        roll_from_ui = -pose_ui.get('roll', 0)

        if self.core_engine.platform_type == '6-DOF':
            offset = self.core_engine.zero_pose_offset
            position = [offset['x'] + pose_ui.get('x', 0)/1000.0, offset['y'] + pose_ui.get('y', 0)/1000.0, h_val + pose_ui.get('z', 0)/1000.0]
            yaw_ui = pose_ui.get('yaw', 0)
            orientation_quat = Rotation.from_euler('ZXY', [yaw_ui, pitch_from_ui, roll_from_ui], degrees=True).as_quat()

        else: # 3-DOF
            position = [0, 0, h_val + pose_ui.get('z', 0)/1000.0]
            orientation_quat = Rotation.from_euler('ZXY', [0, pitch_from_ui, roll_from_ui], degrees=True).as_quat()

        pose_data = {'position': position, 'orientation': orientation_quat}
        if rebuild:
            self.pv_window.rebuild_scene(platform_params, pose_data, labels, reset_view)
        else:
            self.pv_window.update_platform_pose(platform_params, pose_data, labels)

    def toggle_3d_preview_window(self):
        is_visible = self.pv_window.isVisible()
        self.pv_window.setVisible(not is_visible)
        self.toggle_3d_btn.setText("關閉 3D 預覽" if not is_visible else "開啟 3D 預覽")
        if not is_visible:
            self.pv_window.activateWindow()
            self.update_3d_view(rebuild=True)

    def on_save_project(self) -> bool:
        if not self.current_project_path:
            filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "儲存專案", "", "JSON Files (*.json)")
            if not filepath: return False
            self.current_project_path = filepath
        current_view_params = self.pv_window.get_camera_params()
        self.core_engine.update_parameter('view_params', current_view_params)
        if self.core_engine.save_to_file(self.current_project_path):
            self.status_label.setText(f"專案已儲存至 {self.current_project_path}")
            self._set_clean()
            return True
        else:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "錯誤", "專案存檔失敗。")
            return False

    def on_load_project(self):
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "讀取專案", "", "JSON Files (*.json)")
        if not filepath: return
        self._is_loading = True
        try:
            # [最終修正] 還原並優化讀檔邏輯
            # 1. 先建立一個乾淨的 CoreEngine 實例
            self.on_new_project() 
            
            # 2. 直接使用 self.core_engine 載入檔案
            if not self.core_engine.load_from_file(filepath):
                raise IOError("無法從核心引擎讀取檔案。")

            self.current_project_path = filepath
            loaded_type = self.core_engine.platform_type
            
            # 3. 根據讀取到的類型，更新所有 UI 元件
            self.master_geometry_widget.set_platform_type(loaded_type)
            self.controls_stack.setCurrentIndex(0 if loaded_type == "6-DOF" else 1)
            self.dynamics_widget.setEnabled(loaded_type == "6-DOF")
            
            all_params = self.core_engine.get_all_parameters()
            current_widget = self.master_geometry_widget.get_current_platform_widget()
            current_widget.set_input_values(all_params)
            
            if loaded_type == '6-DOF':
                self.dynamics_widget.set_values(all_params)
            
            # 4. 觸發後續的計算與顯示
            self.on_parameters_confirmed(all_params)
            if self.core_engine.get_parameter('H') > 0:
                self.on_calculation_requested(from_load=True)
            
            self._set_clean()
            self.status_label.setText(f"已從 {filepath} 讀取專案。")
            
            self.update_3d_view(rebuild=True, reset_view=False)
            view_params_to_set = self.core_engine.get_parameter('view_params')
            if view_params_to_set:
                self.pv_window.set_camera_params(view_params_to_set)

        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "錯誤", f"專案讀檔失敗: {e}")
            self.on_new_project(platform_type='6-DOF')
        finally:
            self._is_loading = False
            
    def on_dynamics_analysis_requested(self, dynamics_params: dict):
        if self.core_engine.get_parameter('H') <= 0:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "錯誤", "請先成功計算平台零位。")
            return
        for name, value in dynamics_params.items():
            self.core_engine.update_parameter(name, value)
        self._mark_as_dirty()
        base_nodes, mobile_nodes_local = self.core_engine._get_canonical_nodes()
        offset = self.core_engine.zero_pose_offset
        position = [offset['x'], offset['y'], self.core_engine.get_parameter('H')]
        orientation_quat = [0,0,0,1]
        r = Rotation.from_quat(orientation_quat)
        mobile_nodes_world = r.apply(mobile_nodes_local) + np.array(position)
        platform_params = {'nodes_3d_base': base_nodes, 'nodes_3d_mobile_world': mobile_nodes_world, 'position': position, 'orientation_quat': orientation_quat}
        forces = self.dynamics_engine.calculate_actuator_forces(platform_params, dynamics_params)
        self.dynamics_widget.set_result(forces)
        if forces: self.status_label.setText("動力學出力計算完成。")
        else: self.status_label.setText("動力學計算失敗，請檢查參數。")

    def on_pose_changed(self, pose_ui: dict):
        if not self.pv_window.isVisible(): return
        self.update_3d_view(override_pose=pose_ui, rebuild=False)

    def _mark_as_dirty(self):
        if not self.is_dirty: self.is_dirty = True; self._update_window_title()

    def _set_clean(self):
        self.is_dirty = False; self._update_window_title()

    def _update_window_title(self):
        title = self.BASE_TITLE
        if self.current_project_path: title += f" - [{self.current_project_path}]"
        if self.is_dirty: title += " (*)"
        self.setWindowTitle(title)

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        if self.is_dirty:
            reply = QMessageBox.question(self, '關閉程式', "您有未儲存的變更，是否要儲存？", QMessageBox.StandardButton.Save | QMessageBox.StandardButton.Discard | QMessageBox.StandardButton.Cancel)
            if reply == QMessageBox.StandardButton.Save:
                if not self.on_save_project(): event.ignore(); return
            elif reply == QMessageBox.StandardButton.Cancel: event.ignore(); return
        
        if self.pv_window:
             self.pv_window.close()
        
        container = self.window()
        if container is not self and container.parent() is None:
             container.close()

        event.accept()

    def _on_view_button_clicked(self, view_name: str):
        if not self.pv_window.isVisible(): self.toggle_3d_preview_window()
        
        if view_name == 'iso':
            self.update_3d_view(rebuild=True, reset_view=True)
        elif view_name == 'restore_saved': 
            self.pv_window.restore_saved_camera_view()
        else: 
            self.pv_window.handle_view_change(view_name)

# D:\Python_Programs\Stewart_Platform\src\gui\pyvista_widget.py

import pyvista as pv
from pyvistaqt import QtInteractor
import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial.transform import Rotation
from PyQt6.QtCore import pyqtSignal

from ..core import config

class PyVistaWidget(QtInteractor):
    view_changed = pyqtSignal()

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.plotter = self
        
        self.platform_actors = { 
            'base': None, 'mobile': None, 'legs': None, 
            'base_labels': None, 'mobile_labels': None, 
            'base_center': None, 'mobile_center': None 
        }
        self.platform_meshes = { 'base': None, 'mobile': None, 'legs': None }
        
        self.view_history_stack = []
        self.origin_camera_params = None
        self.is_in_preset_view_sequence = False
        self.saved_camera_params = None

        self.iren.add_observer("EndInteractionEvent", self._on_view_interaction_end)
        self.clear_and_init_scene()

    def clear_and_init_scene(self):
        self.plotter.clear()
        self.platform_actors = {key: None for key in self.platform_actors}
        self.platform_meshes = {key: None for key in self.platform_meshes}
        self.view_history_stack.clear()
        self.origin_camera_params = None
        
        self.plotter.add_axes(interactive=True)
        self.plotter.set_background(config.STYLE_BACKGROUND_COLOR)
        # 初始视图将在 rebuild_scene 中设置
        
    def rebuild_scene(self, params: dict, pose: dict, labels: dict, reset_view: bool):
        self.clear_and_init_scene()

        base_nodes = params.get('nodes_3d_base', [])
        mobile_nodes_local = params.get('nodes_3d_mobile', [])
        
        if not base_nodes or not mobile_nodes_local:
            self.plotter.camera_position = 'iso' # Fallback
            return

        position = np.array(pose.get('position', [0,0,0]))
        r = Rotation.from_quat(pose.get('orientation', [0,0,0,1]))
        mobile_nodes_world = r.apply(mobile_nodes_local) + position
        
        self.platform_meshes['base'] = self._create_lines_from_nodes(base_nodes)
        self.platform_meshes['mobile'] = self._create_lines_from_nodes(mobile_nodes_world)
        self.platform_meshes['legs'] = self._create_leg_lines(base_nodes, mobile_nodes_world)
        
        self.platform_actors['base'] = self.plotter.add_mesh(self.platform_meshes['base'], color=config.STYLE_BASE_FRAME_COLOR, line_width=config.STYLE_BASE_FRAME_LINE_WIDTH)
        self.platform_actors['mobile'] = self.plotter.add_mesh(self.platform_meshes['mobile'], color=config.STYLE_MOBILE_FRAME_COLOR, line_width=config.STYLE_MOBILE_FRAME_LINE_WIDTH)
        self.platform_actors['legs'] = self.plotter.add_mesh(self.platform_meshes['legs'], color=config.STYLE_LEG_COLOR, line_width=config.STYLE_LEG_LINE_WIDTH)
        
        label_style = {'point_size': config.STYLE_NODE_POINT_SIZE, 'font_size': config.STYLE_NODE_LABEL_FONT_SIZE, 'text_color': config.STYLE_NODE_LABEL_FONT_COLOR, 'shape_opacity': 0.7, 'render_points_as_spheres': True, 'always_visible': True}
        self.platform_actors['base_labels'] = self.plotter.add_point_labels(np.array(base_nodes), labels.get('base', []), name='base_labels', shape_color=config.STYLE_NODE_LABEL_BASE_BG, **label_style)
        self.platform_actors['mobile_labels'] = self.plotter.add_point_labels(np.array(mobile_nodes_world), labels.get('mobile', []), name='mobile_labels', shape_color=config.STYLE_NODE_LABEL_MOBILE_BG, **label_style)
        
        base_center_pos = np.mean(np.array(base_nodes), axis=0)
        base_center_text = f"({base_center_pos[0]:.3f}, {base_center_pos[1]:.3f}, {base_center_pos[2]:.3f})"
        self.platform_actors['base_center'] = self.plotter.add_point_labels(base_center_pos, [base_center_text], name='base_center_label', point_size=config.STYLE_CENTER_POINT_SIZE, font_size=config.STYLE_CENTER_LABEL_FONT_SIZE, text_color=config.STYLE_CENTER_LABEL_COLOR_BASE, point_color=config.STYLE_CENTER_LABEL_COLOR_BASE, shape_opacity=0.0, always_visible=True, render_points_as_spheres=True)

        mobile_center_pos = np.mean(np.array(mobile_nodes_world), axis=0)
        mobile_center_text = f"({mobile_center_pos[0]:.3f}, {mobile_center_pos[1]:.3f}, {mobile_center_pos[2]:.3f})"
        self.platform_actors['mobile_center'] = self.plotter.add_point_labels(mobile_center_pos, [mobile_center_text], name='mobile_center_label', point_size=config.STYLE_CENTER_POINT_SIZE, font_size=config.STYLE_CENTER_LABEL_FONT_SIZE, text_color=config.STYLE_CENTER_LABEL_COLOR_MOBILE, point_color=config.STYLE_CENTER_LABEL_COLOR_MOBILE, shape_opacity=0.0, always_visible=True, render_points_as_spheres=True)
        
        if reset_view:
            # [修改] 使用手动计算的标准等角视图，而非通用的 'iso'
            # 1. 计算模型的边界框和中心
            bounds = self.plotter.bounds
            center = np.array(self.plotter.center)
            # 2. 计算一个合适的摄像机距离
            diag_length = np.sqrt((bounds[1]-bounds[0])**2 + (bounds[3]-bounds[2])**2 + (bounds[5]-bounds[4])**2)
            distance = diag_length * 2.0
            # 3. 设置摄像机位置在左后上方，焦点为模型中心，确保 Z 轴向上
            camera_pos = center + np.array([-distance, -distance, distance]) / np.sqrt(3)
            focal_point = center
            view_up = [0.0, 0.0, 1.0] # 强制 Z 轴向上
            
            self.plotter.camera_position = [camera_pos, focal_point, view_up]
            self.plotter.camera.zoom(config.CAMERA_ZOOM_FACTOR)
    
    def update_platform_pose(self, params: dict, pose: dict, labels: dict):
        if not all(actor for name, actor in self.platform_actors.items() if name in ['mobile', 'legs']): return
        
        base_nodes = params.get('nodes_3d_base', [])
        mobile_nodes_local = params.get('nodes_3d_mobile', [])
        if not base_nodes or not mobile_nodes_local: return
        
        position = np.array(pose.get('position', [0,0,0]))
        r = Rotation.from_quat(pose.get('orientation', [0,0,0,1]))
        mobile_nodes_world = r.apply(mobile_nodes_local) + position
        
        self.platform_meshes['mobile'].points = mobile_nodes_world
        self.platform_meshes['legs'].points = np.vstack([base_nodes, mobile_nodes_world])
        
        if self.platform_actors.get('mobile_labels'):
            try: self.plotter.remove_actor(self.platform_actors['mobile_labels'], reset_camera=False)
            except (KeyError, ValueError): pass
        if self.platform_actors.get('mobile_center'):
            try: self.plotter.remove_actor(self.platform_actors['mobile_center'], reset_camera=False)
            except (KeyError, ValueError): pass
                
        label_style = {'point_size': config.STYLE_NODE_POINT_SIZE, 'font_size': config.STYLE_NODE_LABEL_FONT_SIZE, 'text_color': config.STYLE_NODE_LABEL_FONT_COLOR, 'shape_opacity': 0.7, 'render_points_as_spheres': True, 'always_visible': True}
        self.platform_actors['mobile_labels'] = self.plotter.add_point_labels(np.array(mobile_nodes_world), labels.get('mobile', []), name='mobile_labels', shape_color=config.STYLE_NODE_LABEL_MOBILE_BG, **label_style)

        mobile_center_pos = np.mean(np.array(mobile_nodes_world), axis=0)
        mobile_center_text = f"({mobile_center_pos[0]:.3f}, {mobile_center_pos[1]:.3f}, {mobile_center_pos[2]:.3f})"
        self.platform_actors['mobile_center'] = self.plotter.add_point_labels(mobile_center_pos, [mobile_center_text], name='mobile_center_label', point_size=config.STYLE_CENTER_POINT_SIZE, font_size=config.STYLE_CENTER_LABEL_FONT_SIZE, text_color=config.STYLE_CENTER_LABEL_COLOR_MOBILE, point_color=config.STYLE_CENTER_LABEL_COLOR_MOBILE, shape_opacity=0.0, always_visible=True, render_points_as_spheres=True)
        
        self.plotter.render()

    def _create_lines_from_nodes(self, nodes):
        if not isinstance(nodes, (np.ndarray, list)) or len(nodes) < 2: return pv.PolyData()
        points = np.array(nodes)
        lines = np.array([[2, i, (i + 1) % len(points)] for i in range(len(points))]).flatten()
        return pv.PolyData(points, lines=lines)
    
    def _create_leg_lines(self, base_nodes, mobile_nodes):
        if not base_nodes or not isinstance(mobile_nodes, np.ndarray) or mobile_nodes.size == 0 or len(base_nodes) != len(mobile_nodes): return pv.PolyData()
        points = np.vstack([base_nodes, mobile_nodes])
        lines = np.array([[2, i, i + len(base_nodes)] for i in range(len(base_nodes))]).flatten()
        return pv.PolyData(points, lines=lines)
        
    def _on_view_interaction_end(self, *args):
        self.is_in_preset_view_sequence = False
        self.view_changed.emit()

    def handle_view_change(self, view_name: str):
        current_params = self.get_camera_params()
        if current_params:
            self.view_history_stack.append(current_params)
            if len(self.view_history_stack) > 20: 
                self.view_history_stack.pop(0)
        if not self.is_in_preset_view_sequence:
            self.origin_camera_params = current_params
        
        if view_name == 'undo': self._restore_from_history()
        elif view_name == 'previous': self._restore_from_origin()
        elif view_name == 'iso':
             # [修改] 调用 rebuild_scene 来重置为我们自定义的等角视图
             # 假设 CoreEngine 的最新几何数据可以被访问
             # 这是一个简化，实际应用可能需要从 MainWindow 触发并传递数据
             self.plotter.clear()
             # 需要一种机制来重新触发带有 reset_view=True 的 rebuild_scene
             print("重置标准等角视图... (需要主视窗协调)")

        else: self._set_preset_view(view_name); self.is_in_preset_view_sequence = True
        self.view_changed.emit()

    def _set_preset_view(self, view_name: str):
        view_methods = {'xy': self.plotter.view_xy, 'xz': self.plotter.view_xz, 'yz': self.plotter.view_yz}
        if view_name in view_methods:
            view_methods[view_name](); self.plotter.camera.zoom(1.2)
            print(f"視角已切換至: {view_name}")

    def _restore_from_history(self):
        if self.view_history_stack:
            self.set_camera_params(self.view_history_stack.pop())
            print("已撤銷至上一步視角。")
        else: print("無更多歷史視角可供撤銷。")

    def _restore_from_origin(self):
        if self.origin_camera_params:
            self.set_camera_params(self.origin_camera_params)
            self.is_in_preset_view_sequence = False
            print("已返回至探索前視角。")
        else: print("沒有記錄探索前視角。")
            
    def closeEvent(self, event):
        self.hide()
        event.accept()

    def get_camera_params(self) -> dict | None:
        if not self.plotter: return None
        cpos = self.plotter.camera_position
        return {'cpos': [list(cpos[0]), list(cpos[1]), list(cpos[2])], 'parallel_scale': self.plotter.camera.parallel_scale}

    def set_camera_params(self, params: dict | None):
        if not params or not self.plotter: return
        if 'cpos' in params and params['cpos']: self.plotter.camera_position = params['cpos']
        if 'parallel_scale' in params: self.plotter.camera.parallel_scale = params['parallel_scale']
        self.saved_camera_params = self.get_camera_params()
        self.plotter.render()
    
    def restore_saved_camera_view(self):
        self.handle_view_change('previous')
        if self.saved_camera_params:
            self.set_camera_params(self.saved_camera_params)
            print("已手動恢復儲存的攝影機視角。")

# D:\Python_Programs\Stewart_Platform\src\gui\controls\analysis_widget.py

import time
import numpy as np
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QGroupBox, QFormLayout, 
                             QLabel, QSlider, QPushButton, QCheckBox, QDoubleSpinBox, 
                             QInputDialog, QMessageBox)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer, QEvent, pyqtSignal
from ...core import config

class AnalysisWidget(QWidget):
    pose_changed = pyqtSignal(dict)
    workspace_analysis_requested = pyqtSignal(str)

    def __init__(self, parent=None, dof_list=['x', 'y', 'z', 'pitch', 'roll', 'yaw']):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.dof_list = dof_list
        self.sliders = {}
        self.workspace_labels = {}
        
        self.animation_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.animation_start_time = 0
        self.animation_duration = config.DEFAULT_ANIMATION_DURATION_S
        self.is_looping_animation = False
        self.selected_dofs_for_animation = []
        
        self._init_ui()
        self._connect_signals()

    def _init_ui(self):
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        
        self.workspace_display_group = self._create_workspace_display()
        self.controls_group = self._create_dof_controls()
        
        main_layout.addWidget(self.workspace_display_group)
        main_layout.addWidget(self.controls_group)

        # 為了簡化訊號連接，不再在此處建立按鈕
        # 按鈕現在統一在 six_dof_widget 和 three_dof_widget 中定義
        
        self.demo_group = self._create_demo_controls()
        main_layout.addWidget(self.demo_group)

        main_layout.addStretch()

    def _connect_signals(self):
        self.animation_timer.timeout.connect(self._update_animation_frame)
        if hasattr(self, 'demo_start_btn'):
            self.demo_start_btn.clicked.connect(self.on_start_demo_clicked)
            self.demo_stop_btn.clicked.connect(self.on_stop_demo_clicked)
            self.demo_loop_checkbox.stateChanged.connect(lambda state: setattr(self, 'is_looping_animation', state == Qt.CheckState.Checked.value))
            self.demo_duration_spinbox.valueChanged.connect(lambda value: setattr(self, 'animation_duration', value))

    def _create_workspace_display(self):
        group = QGroupBox("平台空間範圍") # 修改標題
        form = QFormLayout(group)
        
        dof_map = {
            'x': 'Surge (X, mm)', 'y': 'Sway (Y, mm)', 'z': 'Heave (Z, mm)',
            'pitch': 'Pitch (X, °)', 'roll': 'Roll (Y, °)', 'yaw': 'Yaw (Z, °)'
        }

        for name in self.dof_list:
            label_text = dof_map.get(name, name.capitalize())
            result_label = QLabel("N/A", self)
            form.addRow(label_text, result_label)
            self.workspace_labels[name] = result_label
            
        group.setVisible(False)
        return group

    def _create_dof_controls(self):
        main_group = QGroupBox("平台姿態控制")
        form = QFormLayout(main_group)
        
        dof_map_labels = {
            'x': 'Surge (X)', 'y': 'Sway (Y)', 'z': 'Heave (Z)',
            'pitch': 'Pitch (X)', 'roll': 'Roll (Y)', 'yaw': 'Yaw (Z)'
        }
        
        for name in self.dof_list:
            is_translation = name in ['x', 'y', 'z']
            s_min, s_max, s_init = config.DEFAULT_SLIDER_RANGES.get(name, (-30, 30, 0))
            unit = "mm" if is_translation else "°"
            
            label_text = dof_map_labels.get(name, name.capitalize())
            label = f"{label_text} ({unit})"
            
            self._add_slider_to_form(name, label, s_min, s_max, s_init, form)
        
        return main_group

    def _add_slider_to_form(self, name, label, s_min, s_max, s_init, form_layout):
        slider = QSlider(Qt.Orientation.Horizontal, self)
        prec = config.SLIDER_PRECISION_FACTOR
        slider.setRange(int(s_min * prec), int(s_max * prec))
        slider.setValue(int(s_init * prec))
        slider.installEventFilter(self)

        min_label, max_label, value_label = QLabel(f"{s_min:.1f}"), QLabel(f"{s_max:.1f}"), QLabel(f"{s_init:.1f}")
        checkbox = QCheckBox(self)
        checkbox.setToolTip(f"勾選以將 {label} 加入自動展演")
        
        slider_container = QWidget(self)
        hbox = QHBoxLayout(slider_container); hbox.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        hbox.addWidget(checkbox); hbox.addWidget(min_label); hbox.addWidget(slider, 1); hbox.addWidget(max_label); hbox.addWidget(value_label)
        
        slider.valueChanged.connect(self._on_any_slider_moved)
        self.sliders[name] = {'slider': slider, 'label': value_label, 'min_label': min_label, 'max_label': max_label, 'checkbox': checkbox}
        form_layout.addRow(label, slider_container)
        
    def eventFilter(self, source, event):
        if event.type() == QEvent.Type.MouseButtonDblClick and isinstance(source, QSlider):
            for name, ctrl in self.sliders.items():
                if ctrl['slider'] is source:
                    self._prompt_for_slider_value(name, ctrl)
                    return True
        return super().eventFilter(source, event)

    def _prompt_for_slider_value(self, name, ctrl):
        slider = ctrl['slider']; prec = config.SLIDER_PRECISION_FACTOR
        is_deg = name in ['pitch', 'roll', 'yaw']
        unit = "°" if is_deg else "mm"
        min_val, max_val = slider.minimum() / prec, slider.maximum() / prec
        
        new_value, ok = QInputDialog.getDouble(self, f"精確輸入 - {name}", f"請輸入數值 ({unit}):", slider.value() / prec, min_val, max_val, 2)
        if ok: slider.setValue(int(new_value * prec))

    def _on_any_slider_moved(self):
        pose_ui = {}
        prec = config.SLIDER_PRECISION_FACTOR
        for name, ctrl in self.sliders.items():
            val = ctrl['slider'].value() / prec
            ctrl['label'].setText(f"{val:.1f}")
            pose_ui[name] = val
        self.pose_changed.emit(pose_ui)

    def _create_demo_controls(self):
        group = QGroupBox("姿態展演控制")
        layout = QVBoxLayout(group)
        form_layout = QFormLayout()
        self.demo_duration_spinbox = QDoubleSpinBox(self)
        self.demo_duration_spinbox.setRange(*config.ANIMATION_DURATION_RANGE_S)
        self.demo_duration_spinbox.setValue(self.animation_duration)
        self.demo_duration_spinbox.setSuffix(" s")
        self.demo_loop_checkbox = QCheckBox("循環展演", self)
        form_layout.addRow("展演時間 (秒):", self.demo_duration_spinbox)
        form_layout.addRow(self.demo_loop_checkbox)
        buttons_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.demo_start_btn = QPushButton("開始展演"); self.demo_stop_btn = QPushButton("停止展演")
        self.demo_stop_btn.setEnabled(False)
        buttons_layout.addWidget(self.demo_start_btn); buttons_layout.addWidget(self.demo_stop_btn)
        layout.addLayout(form_layout); layout.addLayout(buttons_layout)
        return group
    
    def on_start_demo_clicked(self):
        if self.animation_timer.isActive(): return
        self.selected_dofs_for_animation = [name for name, ctrl in self.sliders.items() if ctrl['checkbox'].isChecked()]
        if not self.selected_dofs_for_animation:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "提示", "請至少勾選一個要展演的姿態。"); return
        
        self._set_demo_ui_enabled(is_running=True)
        self.animation_start_time = time.time()
        self.animation_timer.start(config.ANIMATION_FRAME_INTERVAL_MS)

    def update_workspace_display(self, limits: dict, analysis_type: str, h_val: float, offset: dict = None):
        if not limits:
            self.workspace_display_group.setVisible(False)
            return

        self.workspace_display_group.setVisible(True)
        is_6dof = 'x' in self.dof_list

        for name in self.dof_list:
            if name not in self.workspace_labels: continue
            label = self.workspace_labels[name]
            min_val = limits.get(f'{name}_min', 0.0)
            max_val = limits.get(f'{name}_max', 0.0)
            unit = 'mm' if name in ['x', 'y', 'z'] else '°'
            
            if unit == 'mm' and name in ['x', 'y']:
                 label.setText(f"[{min_val*1000.0:.1f} ~ {max_val*1000.0:.1f}] {unit}")
            else:
                 label.setText(f"[{min_val:.3f} ~ {max_val:.3f}] {unit}")

        title_suffix = "可用行程範圍" if analysis_type == 'operational' else "機械極限範圍"
        title = f"平台姿態控制 ({title_suffix})"
        self.controls_group.setTitle(title)

        prec = config.SLIDER_PRECISION_FACTOR
        for name, ctrl in self.sliders.items():
            min_v = limits.get(f'{name}_min', -30)
            max_v = limits.get(f'{name}_max', 30)
            
            if is_6dof and offset:
                if name == 'x':
                    min_v = (min_v - offset['x']) * 1000.0
                    max_v = (max_v - offset['x']) * 1000.0
                elif name == 'y':
                    min_v = (min_v - offset['y']) * 1000.0
                    max_v = (max_v - offset['y']) * 1000.0
                elif name == 'z':
                    min_v = (min_v - h_val) * 1000.0
                    max_v = (max_v - h_val) * 1000.0
            
            ctrl['slider'].setRange(int(min_v * prec), int(max_v * prec))
            ctrl['min_label'].setText(f"{min_v:.1f}")
            ctrl['max_label'].setText(f"{max_v:.1f}")
            ctrl['slider'].setValue(0)
            
        self._on_any_slider_moved()

    def reset_workspace_display(self):
        self.workspace_display_group.setVisible(False)
        self.controls_group.setTitle("平台姿態控制")
        for label in self.workspace_labels.values(): label.setText("N/A")

    def on_stop_demo_clicked(self):
        if not self.animation_timer.isActive(): return
        self.animation_timer.stop()
        for name in self.selected_dofs_for_animation: self.sliders[name]['slider'].setValue(0)
        self._on_any_slider_moved()
        self._set_demo_ui_enabled(is_running=False)

    def _update_animation_frame(self):
        elapsed_time = time.time() - self.animation_start_time
        progress = elapsed_time / self.animation_duration
        if progress >= 1.0:
            if self.is_looping_animation:
                progress %= 1.0; self.animation_start_time = time.time()
            else:
                self.on_stop_demo_clicked(); return
        
        if progress < 0.25: norm_val = progress / 0.25
        elif progress < 0.75: norm_val = 1.0 - ((progress - 0.25) / 0.5) * 2.0
        else: norm_val = -1.0 + ((progress - 0.75) / 0.25)
        
        for name in self.selected_dofs_for_animation:
            slider = self.sliders[name]['slider']
            slider.blockSignals(True)
            if norm_val >= 0: slider.setValue(int(norm_val * slider.maximum()))
            else: slider.setValue(int(-norm_val * slider.minimum()))
            slider.blockSignals(False)
        self._on_any_slider_moved()

    def _set_demo_ui_enabled(self, is_running: bool):
        if not hasattr(self, 'demo_start_btn'): return
        self.demo_start_btn.setEnabled(not is_running)
        self.demo_stop_btn.setEnabled(is_running)
        self.demo_duration_spinbox.setEnabled(not is_running)
        self.demo_loop_checkbox.setEnabled(not is_running)
        for ctrl in self.sliders.values():
            ctrl['slider'].setEnabled(not is_running)
            ctrl['checkbox'].setEnabled(not is_running)
